FEATURE-ReportDaily.md ===\n# FEATURE: Report Daily\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261026 V1 |\n| Description | Writes the daily CSV report with one row per start date and a TOTAL row. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md, FEATURE-CliExit.md, FEATURE-ValueFormat.md |\n| Provides    | ddkstat daily |\n| Consumes    | cli.Register, format.Minutes, logread.Record |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nThe `daily` report shows, for each start date, how many calls ran, how many failed, how many tokens they used, and how many minutes they took.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Header: `date,calls,failed,tokens,minutes`.\n- The report covers the records that remain after the filters are applied.\n- One row per start date (the first 10 characters of `started_at`), in ascending byte order.\n- `calls` is the record count. `failed` is the count of records whose status is not `succeeded`.\n- `tokens` is input plus cached plus output tokens, summed over the group.\n- `minutes` is the group's summed `elapsed_ms` formatted by `format.Minutes`.\n- A final `TOTAL` row covers all filtered records.\n- When no record matches, the report writes the header and a `TOTAL` row of zeros with `0.0` minutes.\n- Aggregation (`AggregateDaily`) and writing (`WriteDaily`) are separate functions in `internal/report`.\n- The writer is registered with `cli.Register(\"daily\", ...)`.\n- Output is CSV, `\\n` line endings, values unquoted.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC report-daily-unit ===\nIntent: The native unit tests for daily aggregation, ordering, totals, empty input and writing all pass.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nfor name in (\n    \"TestDailyOrdering\",\n    \"TestDailyTokensSum\",\n    \"TestDailyTotal\",\n    \"TestDailyEmpty\",\n    \"TestDailyWrite\",\n):\n    result = run_test(\"./internal/report\", name)\n    print(name, result.status)\n    assert result.passed, (name, result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC report-daily-unit ===\n\n=== AC report-daily-rows ===\nIntent: The daily command emits one row per start date with calls, Decimal\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\n\ndef rec(n, started, cmd, target, status, tin, tc, tout, ms):\n    return {\n        \"schema_version\": 1,\n        \"execution_id\": \"id-%03d\" % \"log.jsonl\")\nwith open(path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as fh:\n    for r in recs:\n        fh.write(json.dumps(r) + \"\\n\")\n\n\ndef expected(group):\n    total_ms = Description | Writes the failures CSV report listing each failed record in start order. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md, FEATURE-CliExit.md, FEATURE-ValueFormat.md |\n| Provides    | ddkstat failures |\n| Consumes    | cli.Register, logread.Record |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nThe `failures` report lists every failed call so the operator can see which command, Target and model failed, and how.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Header: `started_at,target,command,model,returncode,timed_out`.\n- One row per failed record (status is not `succeeded`) among the filtered records.\n- Rows are ordered by `started_at`, then `execution_id`, ascending by byte value.\n- `started_at` is written exactly as logged.\n- `model` is `Record.Model`: the executed model when present and non-empty, otherwise the requested model.\n- `returncode` is empty when null or absent, otherwise the integer.\n- `timed_out` is `true` or `false`.\n- There is no `TOTAL` row. When no failed record matches, only the header is written.\n- Selection and ordering (`SelectFailures`) and writing (`WriteFailures`) are separate functions in `internal/report`.\n- The writer is registered with `cli.Register(\"failures\", ...)`.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC report-failures-unit ===\nIntent: The native unit tests for failure selection, ordering, null returncode, timed_out text and empty output all pass.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nfor name in (\n    \"TestFailuresOrdering\",\n    \"TestFailuresNullReturncodeEmpty\",\n    \"TestFailuresTimedOutText\",\n    \"TestFailuresOnlyFailed\",\n    \"TestFailuresEmpty\",\n):\n    result = run_test(\"./internal/report\", name)\n    print(name, result.status)\n    assert result.passed, (name, result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC report-failures-unit ===\n\n=== AC report-failures-rows ===\nIntent: The failures command lists only failed records ordered by started_at then execution_id, with model fallback, empty null returncode and true/false timed_out.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nimport json\nimport os\nimport tempfile\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\n\ndef rec(eid, started, target, cmd, status, rc, timed_out, stats_model):\n    stats = logread.Record |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nThe `daily` report shows, for each start date, how many calls ran, how many failed, how many tokens they used, and how many minutes they took.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Header: `date,calls,failed,tokens,minutes`.\n- The report covers the records that remain after the filters are applied.\n- One row per start date (the first 10 characters of `started_at`), in ascending byte order.\n- `calls` is the record count. `failed` is the count of records whose status is not `succeeded`.\n- `tokens` is input plus cached plus output tokens, summed over the group.\n- `minutes` is the group's summed `elapsed_ms` formatted by `format.Minutes`.\n- A final `TOTAL` row covers all filtered records.\n- When no record matches, the report writes the header and a `TOTAL` row of zeros with `0.0` minutes.\n- Aggregation (`AggregateDaily`) and writing (`WriteDaily`) are separate functions in `internal/report`.\n- The writer is registered with
